// 保持旧版变量名兼容性
const EPOCHS_TWO_TO_EIGHT_DATABASE = EPOCHS_TWO_TO_TEN_DATABASE;

/**
 * 推进执行某个指定纪元的某个事件
 */
function runSpecificEpochEvent(epochIndex, eventIdx, optionIdx, ancestorState) {
  const epochData = EPOCHS_TWO_TO_TEN_DATABASE.find(e => e.epochIndex === epochIndex);
  if (!epochData) return null;
  const evt = epochData.historicalEvents[eventIdx];
  if (!evt) return null;
  const opt = evt.options[optionIdx];
  if (!opt) return null;

  const result = {
    epochIndex,
    epochName: epochData.epochName,
    eventId: evt.id,
    eventTitle: evt.title,
    eventYear: evt.year,
    chosenText: opt.text,
    success: true,
    chronicleSnippet: ''
  };

  // 1. 属性检定 (支持 reqStat 与 reqTrack 多维赛道映射)
  let statKey = opt.reqStat;
  if (!statKey && opt.reqTrack) {
    const tr = String(opt.reqTrack).toLowerCase();
    if (['official', 'gov', 'politics'].includes(tr)) statKey = 'rep';
    else if (['scholar', 'edu', 'study'].includes(tr)) statKey = 'iq';
    else if (['merchant', 'biz', 'trade'].includes(tr)) statKey = 'iq';
    else if (['military', 'soldier'].includes(tr)) statKey = 'courage';
    else if (['recluse', 'hermit'].includes(tr)) statKey = 'clean';
    else if (['medical', 'doctor', 'herb'].includes(tr)) statKey = 'health';
    else statKey = 'courage';
  }
  if (statKey && typeof opt.reqVal === 'number') {
    const val = ancestorState[statKey] !== undefined ? ancestorState[statKey] : (ancestorState.courage || 50);
    if (val < opt.reqVal) {
      result.success = false;
      const pen = opt.failPenalty || {};
      for (const k in pen) {
        if (k !== 'chronicleText') ancestorState[k] = (ancestorState[k] || 0) + pen[k];
      }
      result.chronicleSnippet = pen.chronicleText || '世途多艰，遭逢坎坷。';
      return result;
    }
  }

  // 2. 扣除成本
  if (opt.cost) {
    for (const k in opt.cost) {
      ancestorState[k] = Math.max(0, (ancestorState[k] || 0) - opt.cost[k]);
    }
  }

  // 3. 施加收益
  const rew = opt.successReward || {};
  for (const k in rew) {
    if (k !== 'chronicleText' && k !== 'relicId' && k !== 'officialTitle' && k !== 'newMottoTag' && k !== 'branchTag') {
      ancestorState[k] = (ancestorState[k] || 0) + rew[k];
    }
  }

  if (rew.officialTitle) ancestorState.officialTitle = rew.officialTitle;
  if (rew.relicId) ancestorState.relicId = rew.relicId;
  if (rew.newMottoTag) {
    ancestorState.mottoTags = ancestorState.mottoTags || [];
    ancestorState.mottoTags.push(rew.newMottoTag);
  }

  result.chronicleSnippet = rew.chronicleText || '秉祖德以行，克绍箕裘。';
  return result;
}

/**
 * 结算纪元二至十终局先祖卡片
 */
function settleEpochAncestorRecord(epochIndex, familyProfile, ancestorState, decisionHistory = []) {
  if (typeof epochIndex === 'object' && typeof familyProfile === 'number') {
    const temp = epochIndex;
    epochIndex = familyProfile;
    familyProfile = temp;
  }
  familyProfile = familyProfile || {};
  ancestorState = ancestorState || {};
  decisionHistory = decisionHistory || [];

  const epochData = EPOCHS_TWO_TO_TEN_DATABASE.find(e => e.epochIndex === epochIndex);
  const DEFAULT_EPOCH_NAMES = [
    '', '汉魏风骨', '六朝烟雨', '南北归一', '盛唐气象', '宋韵风华',
    '两宋烽烟', '大元一统', '阳明心学', '康乾鼎盛', '近代风雷',
    '近地开发', '深空拓荒', '行星生态', '恒星远航', '星际长歌'
  ];
  const epochName = epochData ? epochData.epochName : (DEFAULT_EPOCH_NAMES[epochIndex] || `纪元${epochIndex}`);
  const timeSpan = epochData ? epochData.timeSpan : [100 + (epochIndex - 1) * 200, 100 + epochIndex * 200];

  // 动态依据经历关隘年份计算生卒享年
  const years = decisionHistory.map(d => d.eventYear || d.year).filter(y => typeof y === 'number');
  const minYear = years.length > 0 ? Math.min(...years) : timeSpan[0] + 15;
  const maxYear = years.length > 0 ? Math.max(...years) : timeSpan[1] - 20;
  const birthYear = ancestorState.birthYear || Math.max(timeSpan[0], minYear - 18);
  const deathYear = ancestorState.deathYear || Math.min(timeSpan[1], Math.max(birthYear + 60, maxYear + 5));
  const realisticLifespan = [birthYear, deathYear];

  // 按家族姓氏与家业底色拟定多元名讳
  const EPOCH_NAME_CANDIDATES = {
    2: [{ name: '彦先', zi: '伯达' }, { name: '承安', zi: '季雅' }, { name: '元规', zi: '仲舒' }],
    3: [{ name: '崇礼', zi: '孝先' }, { name: '开皇', zi: '化远' }, { name: '延龄', zi: '公寿' }],
    4: [{ name: '承业', zi: '宏道' }, { name: '知微', zi: '守礼' }, { name: '元崇', zi: '思敬' }],
    5: [{ name: '希贤', zi: '德基' }, { name: '正卿', zi: '纯夫' }, { name: '至德', zi: '善长' }],
    6: [{ name: '忠恕', zi: '子与' }, { name: '克绍', zi: '文伯' }, { name: '伯符', zi: '君仪' }],
    7: [{ name: '廷辅', zi: '良谟' }, { name: '嘉谟', zi: '子猷' }, { name: '世恩', zi: '若愚' }],
    8: [{ name: '守愚', zi: '笃卿' }, { name: '述圣', zi: '维新' }, { name: '培英', zi: '济生' }],
    9: [{ name: '济苍', zi: '砥平' }, { name: '靖宇', zi: '自强' }, { name: '秉钧', zi: '辅邦' }],
    10: [{ name: '新民', zi: '振华' }, { name: '志远', zi: '海潮' }, { name: '立德', zi: '思源' }],
    11: [{ name: '天行', zi: '纪宇' }, { name: '晨曦', zi: '启明' }, { name: '贯日', zi: '凌云' }],
    12: [{ name: '远航', zi: '拓星' }, { name: '巡天', zi: '御宇' }, { name: '越海', zi: '扶摇' }],
    13: [{ name: '泰初', zi: '守元' }, { name: '玄同', zi: '应变' }, { name: '造极', zi: '见素' }],
    14: [{ name: '开阳', zi: '渡虚' }, { name: '浩然', zi: '载驰' }, { name: '太虚', zi: '澄空' }],
    15: [{ name: '太和', zi: '同光' }, { name: '大同', zi: '归元' }, { name: '三千', zi: '永纪' }]
  };

  const seed = (familyProfile.surname ? familyProfile.surname.charCodeAt(0) : 7) + epochIndex;
  const candList = EPOCH_NAME_CANDIDATES[epochIndex] || [{ name: '彦先', zi: '伯达' }];
  const picked = candList[seed % candList.length];

  const givenName = ancestorState.name || picked.name;
  const ziHao = ancestorState.ziHao || picked.zi;
  const official = ancestorState.officialTitle || '布衣名士';

  // 综合数值与家业底色拟定谥号
  const voc = familyProfile.vocationId || 'study_scholar';
  let posthumous = '庄惠公';
  if (voc === 'trade_craft') {
    posthumous = (ancestorState.money || 0) > 40000 ? '通惠公' : '裕民公';
  } else if (voc === 'medical_herb') {
    posthumous = (ancestorState.morality || 50) > 70 ? '仁康公' : '普济公';
  } else if (voc === 'iron_craft') {
    posthumous = (ancestorState.courage || 50) > 70 ? '格致公' : '工敏公';
  } else {
    // study_scholar
    if ((ancestorState.morality || 50) > 75) posthumous = '文正公';
    else if ((ancestorState.iq || 50) > 75) posthumous = '文简公';
    else if ((ancestorState.courage || 50) > 75) posthumous = '武烈公';
    else posthumous = '庄惠公';
  }

  // 拟定本纪元传承家训
  let newMottoText = '守正出奇，顺应时势。敦宗睦族，行善积德。';
  if (ancestorState.mottoTags && ancestorState.mottoTags.includes('义庄义学')) {
    newMottoText = '贫富相恤，义庄永昌。族中有秀，合力扶持。';
  } else if (ancestorState.mottoTags && ancestorState.mottoTags.includes('诚贾立基')) {
    newMottoText = '通商有道，信用第一。以诚聚财，以仁分财。';
  } else if (ancestorState.mottoTags && ancestorState.mottoTags.includes('知行合一')) {
    newMottoText = '知行合一，致其良知。事上磨练，不欺暗室。';
  } else if (ancestorState.mottoTags && ancestorState.mottoTags.includes('敢为人先')) {
    newMottoText = '勇立潮头，敢为人先。实干兴家，科技自立。';
  }

  const deeds = decisionHistory.map(d => `【${d.eventYear}年】${d.chronicleSnippet}`);

  return {
    eraIndex: epochIndex,
    eraName: epochName,
    givenName,
    ziHao,
    lifespan: realisticLifespan,
    officialTitle: official,
    posthumousTitle: posthumous,
    epitaph: `身逢【${epochName}】风云之会，秉祖德周旋于大争之世。修明家规，化解兵燹险阻，护佑宗支长青。`,
    finalDeeds: deeds,
    bequeathedMoney: Math.max(2000, Math.floor(ancestorState.money || 5000)),
    prestigeGain: ancestorState.clanPrestige || 50,
    newMotto: {
      era: `第${epochIndex}世·${epochName}留训`,
      text: newMottoText,
      author: `${familyProfile.surname}公${givenName}`
    },
    passedRelic: ancestorState.relicId ? { id: ancestorState.relicId, name: `${epochName}传家信物` } : null
  };
}

if (typeof module !== 'undefined' && module.exports) {
  module.exports = {
    EPOCHS_TWO_TO_TEN_DATABASE,
    EPOCHS_TWO_TO_EIGHT_DATABASE,
    runSpecificEpochEvent,
    settleEpochAncestorRecord
  };
}

  ...((ERA_HISTORICAL_CHILDHOOD[100] || []).filter(e => e.age > 5))

<file path="wechat-minigame/src/data/clanSocialSystem.js">
// 华夏三千年世家大系 · 社交裂变与宗族互联系统 (ClanSocialSystem)
// 包含：百家姓氏榜、八方籍贯榜、家业底色榜、联姻结世交裂变引擎、宗亲会与同乡会中枢

const MAX_ALLIANCES_TOTAL = 12;
const MAX_ALLIANCES_PER_DAY = 3;

function getTodayDateString() {
  const d = new Date();
  return `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

/**
 * 1. 榜单聚合计算引擎
 */
function buildSurnameLeaderboard(familyList = []) {
  const map = {};
  familyList.forEach(fam => {
    const s = fam.surname || '无名';
    if (!map[s]) {
      map[s] = {
        surname: s,
        totalPrestige: 0,
        totalTrustFund: 0,
        clanCount: 0,
        representativeHall: fam.hallName
      };
    }
    map[s].totalPrestige += (fam.clanTrust ? fam.clanTrust.prestigeScore : 0);
    map[s].totalTrustFund += (fam.clanTrust ? fam.clanTrust.trustFundMoney : 0);
    map[s].clanCount += 1;
  });

  return Object.values(map).sort((a, b) => b.totalPrestige - a.totalPrestige);
}

function buildProvinceLeaderboard(familyList = []) {
  const map = {};
  familyList.forEach(fam => {
    const p = fam.province || '中原';
    if (!map[p]) {
      map[p] = {
        province: p,
        totalPrestige: 0,
        clanCount: 0,
        topSurname: fam.surname
      };
    }
    map[p].totalPrestige += (fam.clanTrust ? fam.clanTrust.prestigeScore : 0);
    map[p].clanCount += 1;
  });

  return Object.values(map).sort((a, b) => b.totalPrestige - a.totalPrestige);
}

function buildVocationLeaderboard(familyList = []) {
  const map = {};
  familyList.forEach(fam => {
    const voc = fam.vocationId || 'study_scholar';
    const vocName = fam.vocationName || '耕读传家';
    if (!map[voc]) {
      map[voc] = {
        vocationId: voc,
        vocationName: vocName,
        totalPrestige: 0,
        clanCount: 0
      };
    }
    map[voc].totalPrestige += (fam.clanTrust ? fam.clanTrust.prestigeScore : 0);
    map[voc].clanCount += 1;
  });

  return Object.values(map).sort((a, b) => b.totalPrestige - a.totalPrestige);
}

/**
 * 2. 联姻与结为世交裂变引擎 (Invitation & Alliance Engine)
 */
const ALLIANCE_SECRET_SALT = 'LIFESIM_ALLIANCE_TOKEN_SALT_2026';

function generateAllianceToken(fromFamId, fromSurname, fromProvince, timestamp, nonce) {
  const str = `${fromFamId}:${fromSurname}:${fromProvince}:${timestamp}:${nonce}:${ALLIANCE_SECRET_SALT}`;
  let hash = 0x811c9dc5;
  for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {
    hash ^= str.charCodeAt(i);
    hash = Math.imul(hash, 0x01000193);
  }
  return (hash >>> 0).toString(16);
}

/**
 * 2. 联姻与结为世交裂变引擎 (Invitation & Alliance Engine)
 */
function generateMarriageInvitationCard(myFamily, youthName = '长子') {
  if (!myFamily) return null;

  const ts = Date.now();
  const nonce = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10);
  const token = generateAllianceToken(myFamily.familyId, myFamily.surname, myFamily.province, ts, nonce);

  return {
    shareTitle: `【世家结秦晋之好】${myFamily.province}·${myFamily.surname}氏诚备庚帖，邀君共立百年通好！`,
    shareDesc: `我族乃${myFamily.hallName}，已历${myFamily.genealogyList.length || 1}代风霜，特邀世交名门联姻互利！`,
    queryParam: `action=alliance&fromFamId=${myFamily.familyId}&fromSurname=${encodeURIComponent(myFamily.surname)}&fromProvince=${encodeURIComponent(myFamily.province)}&fromHall=${encodeURIComponent(myFamily.hallName)}&ts=${ts}&nonce=${nonce}&token=${token}`,
    inviterData: {
      familyId: myFamily.familyId,
      fullName: myFamily.fullName,
      surname: myFamily.surname,
      hallName: myFamily.hallName,
      province: myFamily.province,
      youthName: youthName,
      generation: myFamily.genealogyList.length || 1,
      ts,
      nonce,
      token
    }
  };
}

function _reportValidationFailure(res, invite) {
  if (typeof reportGameMilestone === 'function') {
    const payload = {
      reason: res.reason,
      message: res.message,
      fromFamId: (invite && invite.fromFamId) || '',
      fromSurname: (invite && invite.fromSurname) || '',
      fromProvince: (invite && invite.fromProvince) || ''
    };
    reportGameMilestone('marriage_validation_failed', payload);
    reportGameMilestone('marriage_verify_failed', payload);
  }
}

/**
 * 校验外部传入的联姻邀请庚帖合法性 (防刷、防伪造、防自恋同盟、防重放、时效校验)
 */
function validateMarriageInvite(invite, myFamily, networkTimestamp = null) {
  if (!invite || typeof invite !== 'object') {
    const res = { valid: false, reason: 'INVALID_DATA', message: '世家庚帖数据不完整' };
    _reportValidationFailure(res, invite);
    return res;
  }
  if (!myFamily) {
    const res = { valid: false, reason: 'NO_MY_FAMILY', message: '尚未立族，无法缔结世交' };
    _reportValidationFailure(res, invite);
    return res;
  }
  const sName = invite.fromSurname && typeof invite.fromSurname === 'string' ? invite.fromSurname.trim() : '';
  const prov = invite.fromProvince && typeof invite.fromProvince === 'string' ? invite.fromProvince.trim() : '';
  const hall = invite.fromHall && typeof invite.fromHall === 'string' ? invite.fromHall.trim() : '';

  if (!sName || !/^[\u4e00-\u9fa5]{1,2}$/.test(sName)) {
    const res = { valid: false, reason: 'INVALID_SURNAME', message: '求聘方姓氏不合规' };
    _reportValidationFailure(res, invite);
    return res;
  }
  if (!prov || prov.length > 8) {
    const res = { valid: false, reason: 'INVALID_PROVINCE', message: '求聘方籍贯不合规' };
    _reportValidationFailure(res, invite);
    return res;
  }
  if (!hall || hall.length > 12) {
    const res = { valid: false, reason: 'INVALID_HALL', message: '求聘方堂号不合规' };
    _reportValidationFailure(res, invite);
    return res;
  }
  if (invite.fromFamId && invite.fromFamId === myFamily.familyId) {
    const res = { valid: false, reason: 'SELF_ALLIANCE', message: '不可与同族自身结为世交' };
    _reportValidationFailure(res, invite);
    return res;
  }
  if (sName === myFamily.surname && prov === myFamily.province && hall === myFamily.hallName) {
    const res = { valid: false, reason: 'SAME_CLAN_BRANCH', message: '同堂同籍本家无须重复缔结秦晋之好' };
    _reportValidationFailure(res, invite);
    return res;
  }

  // 1. 签名与印信防伪防篡改校验 (强制要求 Token、时间戳与 Nonce，严禁无印信绕过)
  if (!invite.token || !invite.ts || !invite.nonce) {
    const res = { valid: false, reason: 'MISSING_TOKEN', message: '庚帖防伪印信缺失，无法缔结世交' };
    _reportValidationFailure(res, invite);
    return res;
  }
  const expectedToken = generateAllianceToken(invite.fromFamId, sName, prov, invite.ts, invite.nonce);
  if (invite.token !== expectedToken) {
    const res = { valid: false, reason: 'INVALID_SIGNATURE', message: '庚帖印信防伪校验未通过（疑似伪造）' };
    _reportValidationFailure(res, invite);
    return res;
  }

  // 2. 时效性与网络授时校验 (防时间穿越与重放攻击)
  const now = (typeof networkTimestamp === 'number' && networkTimestamp > 0) ? networkTimestamp : Date.now();
  if (invite.ts) {
    const inviteAge = now - Number(invite.ts);
    if (inviteAge > 7 * 86400 * 1000) {
      const res = { valid: false, reason: 'EXPIRED_INVITE', message: '世家庚帖已过时效（有效限7日）' };
      _reportValidationFailure(res, invite);
      return res;
    }
    if (inviteAge < -3600 * 1000) {
      const res = { valid: false, reason: 'FUTURE_INVITE', message: '庚帖时序异常，请校准网络时间' };
      _reportValidationFailure(res, invite);
      return res;
    }
  }

  // 3. 幂等防重放：已领受过的 Nonce 不得重复领受
  myFamily._redeemedInviteNonces = myFamily._redeemedInviteNonces || [];
  if (invite.nonce && myFamily._redeemedInviteNonces.includes(invite.nonce)) {
    const res = { valid: false, reason: 'ALREADY_REDEEMED', message: '此道庚帖已被兑纳，不可重复领受' };
    _reportValidationFailure(res, invite);
    return res;
  }

  myFamily.alliances = myFamily.alliances || [];
  if (myFamily.alliances.length >= MAX_ALLIANCES_TOTAL) {
    const res = { valid: false, reason: 'MAX_ALLIANCES_EXCEEDED', message: `世交名额已达上限（最多${MAX_ALLIANCES_TOTAL}家）` };
    _reportValidationFailure(res, invite);
    return res;
  }

  const already = myFamily.alliances.some(a =>
    (invite.fromFamId && a.targetFamilyId === invite.fromFamId) ||
    (a.surname === sName && a.province === prov && a.hallName === hall)
  );
  if (already) {
    const res = { valid: false, reason: 'ALREADY_ALLIED', message: '两家此前已结为通好世交' };
    _reportValidationFailure(res, invite);
    return res;
  }

  // 4. 设备时间防倒拨作弊检测 (Anti Clock Tamper)
  myFamily._highestSeenNetworkTs = Math.max(myFamily._highestSeenNetworkTs || 0, now);
  if (now < myFamily._highestSeenNetworkTs - 86400 * 1000) {
    const res = { valid: false, reason: 'TIME_TAMPER_DETECTED', message: '检测到设备系统时间异常，请对齐网络授时！' };
    _reportValidationFailure(res, invite);
    return res;
  }

  const today = getTodayDateString();
  const dailyRecord = myFamily._dailyAllianceRecord || { date: today, count: 0 };
  if (dailyRecord.date === today && dailyRecord.count >= MAX_ALLIANCES_PER_DAY) {
    const res = { valid: false, reason: 'DAILY_LIMIT_EXCEEDED', message: `今日缔结世交已达上限（每日限${MAX_ALLIANCES_PER_DAY}次）` };
    _reportValidationFailure(res, invite);
    return res;
  }

  return { valid: true };
}

/**
 * 收益动态阶梯衰减算法 (Diminishing Returns)
 * 每日第 1 次：+25 声望，+2500 礼金
 * 每日第 2 次：+15 声望，+1500 礼金
 * 每日第 3 次：+8 声望，+800 礼金
 * 每日第 4+ 次：0 收益（仅记录盟约关系）
 */
function calculateAllianceReward(dailyIndex) {
  if (dailyIndex === 0) return { prestige: 25, money: 2500 };
  if (dailyIndex === 1) return { prestige: 15, money: 1500 };
  if (dailyIndex === 2) return { prestige: 8, money: 800 };
  return { prestige: 0, money: 0 };
}

/**
 * 双向缔结世家通好/联姻盟约 (安全闭环校验与阶梯衰减收益)
 */
function acceptMarriageAlliance(familyA, familyB, options = {}) {
  if (!familyA || !familyB) {
    return { success: false, reason: 'INCOMPLETE_DATA', message: '联姻双方世家数据不完整' };
  }
  if (familyA.familyId === familyB.familyId) {
    return { success: false, reason: 'SELF_ALLIANCE', message: '不可与同族自身结为世交' };
  }
  if (familyA.surname === familyB.surname && familyA.province === familyB.province && familyA.hallName === familyB.hallName) {
    return { success: false, reason: 'SAME_BRANCH', message: '同堂同籍本家无须重复缔结秦晋之好' };
  }

  familyA.alliances = familyA.alliances || [];
  familyB.alliances = familyB.alliances || [];

  if (familyA.alliances.length >= MAX_ALLIANCES_TOTAL) {
    return { success: false, reason: 'MAX_ALLIANCES_EXCEEDED', message: `世家通好盟约已达上限（最多${MAX_ALLIANCES_TOTAL}门世交）` };
  }

  const today = getTodayDateString();
  familyA._dailyAllianceRecord = familyA._dailyAllianceRecord || { date: today, count: 0 };
  if (familyA._dailyAllianceRecord.date !== today) {
    familyA._dailyAllianceRecord = { date: today, count: 0 };
  }
  if (familyA._dailyAllianceRecord.count >= MAX_ALLIANCES_PER_DAY) {
    return { success: false, reason: 'DAILY_LIMIT_EXCEEDED', message: `今日缔结世交已达上限（每日限${MAX_ALLIANCES_PER_DAY}次）` };
  }

  // 防重复结盟
  const existsA = familyA.alliances.some(a =>
    a.targetFamilyId === familyB.familyId ||
    (a.surname === familyB.surname && a.province === familyB.province && a.hallName === familyB.hallName)
  );
  if (existsA) {
    return { success: false, reason: 'ALREADY_ALLIED', message: '两家此前已结为通好世交！' };
  }

  const bondYear = familyA.timeline ? familyA.timeline.currentYear : 2026;

  if (!familyA.clanTrust) familyA.clanTrust = { prestigeScore: 50, trustFundMoney: 5000 };
  if (!familyB.clanTrust) familyB.clanTrust = { prestigeScore: 50, trustFundMoney: 5000 };

  // 1. 双向写入世交盟约
  familyA.alliances.push({
    targetFamilyId: familyB.familyId,
    fullName: familyB.fullName || `${familyB.province}·${familyB.surname}氏·${familyB.hallName}`,
    surname: familyB.surname,
    province: familyB.province,
    hallName: familyB.hallName,
    bondYear: bondYear,
    allianceBuff: { mutualAidBonus: 0.15 } // 危机与经商获利 15% 互相加成
  });

  familyB.alliances.push({
    targetFamilyId: familyA.familyId,
    fullName: familyA.fullName || `${familyA.province}·${familyA.surname}氏·${familyA.hallName}`,
    surname: familyA.surname,
    province: familyA.province,
    hallName: familyA.hallName,
    bondYear: bondYear,
    allianceBuff: { mutualAidBonus: 0.15 }
  });

  // 2. 动态收益阶梯衰减：杜绝联姻暴富与脚本刷分
  const dailyIdx = familyA._dailyAllianceRecord.count;
  const reward = calculateAllianceReward(dailyIdx);

  familyA.clanTrust.prestigeScore = (familyA.clanTrust.prestigeScore || 0) + reward.prestige;
  familyB.clanTrust.prestigeScore = (familyB.clanTrust.prestigeScore || 0) + reward.prestige;
  familyA.clanTrust.trustFundMoney = (familyA.clanTrust.trustFundMoney || 0) + reward.money;
  familyB.clanTrust.trustFundMoney = (familyB.clanTrust.trustFundMoney || 0) + reward.money;

  // 记录每日计数
  familyA._dailyAllianceRecord.count += 1;

  // 记录已兑纳 Nonce，杜绝重放
  if (options.nonce) {
    familyA._redeemedInviteNonces = familyA._redeemedInviteNonces || [];
    familyA._redeemedInviteNonces.push(options.nonce);
    if (familyA._redeemedInviteNonces.length > 50) {
      familyA._redeemedInviteNonces = familyA._redeemedInviteNonces.slice(-50);
    }
  }

  // 3. 载入双方纪事录
  const chronicleTextA = `【世家盟好】与【${familyB.province}·${familyB.surname}氏·${familyB.hallName}】喜结秦晋之好，互通商路，声望+${reward.prestige}，礼金+¥${reward.money}！`;
  const chronicleTextB = `【世家盟好】与【${familyA.province}·${familyA.surname}氏·${familyA.hallName}】喜结秦晋之好，共立百代同盟誓约！`;

  return {
    success: true,
    bondYear,
    familyA_allianceCount: familyA.alliances.length,
    familyB_allianceCount: familyB.alliances.length,
    rewardPrestige: reward.prestige,
    rewardMoney: reward.money,
    chronicleTextA,
    chronicleTextB
  };
}

/**
 * 3. 同姓宗亲会与同乡世交总公所
 */
function getClanAssociationDetails(family) {
  if (!family) return null;

  return {
    clanGuild: {
      name: `全国${family.surname}氏宗亲总联合会`,
      desc: `汇聚天下${family.surname}氏血脉风骨，同宗同源，一脉相承。`,
      privileges: ['同姓互赠宗祠香火（每日领信托增益）', '宗谱联修参阅权', '百家姓氏总榜计分']
    },
    homelandGuild: {
      name: `${family.province}乡邦世家联谊公所`,
      desc: `聚集${family.province}本土望族，乡情融洽，共御水旱天灾。`,
      privileges: ['乡邦赈济储备金借贷', '地方风俗大考答辩加成', '八方籍贯总榜争鸣']
    }
  };
}

if (typeof module !== 'undefined' && module.exports) {
  module.exports = {
    buildSurnameLeaderboard,
    buildProvinceLeaderboard,
    buildVocationLeaderboard,
    generateMarriageInvitationCard,
    generateAllianceToken,
    calculateAllianceReward,
    validateMarriageInvite,
    acceptMarriageAlliance,
    getClanAssociationDetails
  };
}

<file path="wechat-minigame/src/data/contentUpdater.js">
/**
 * 《风华年代：沧海沉浮录》线上内容热更新引擎 (Content Hot Update Engine)
 * 职责：
 * 1. 严格遵守微信小游戏平台合规规范：绝不动态执行任何外部 JS 代码，仅拉取纯 JSON 数据；
 * 2. 支持版本比对、本地持久化缓存、离线秒开、网络抖动降级；
 * 3. 将 CDN 下发的 JSON 增量剧本、文案表、突发事件与数值补丁热合并到运行态数据对象中。
 */

let _currentContentVersion = '2026.10.15.v1';
let _localManifest = null;
try {
  _localManifest = require('./content_manifest.json');
} catch (_) {
  _localManifest = { manifestVersion: '2026.10.15.v1' };
}

function getContentVersion() {
  return _currentContentVersion;
}

// 版本大小比对函数 (支持时间戳版本如 2026.10.15.v1 或语义化版本 1.0.2)
function compareVersions(v1, v2) {
  if (!v1 && !v2) return 0;
  if (!v1) return -1;
  if (!v2) return 1;
  const p1 = String(v1).replace(/^v/, '').split(/[.\-_v]/).map(n => isNaN(Number(n)) ? n : Number(n));
  const p2 = String(v2).replace(/^v/, '').split(/[.\-_v]/).map(n => isNaN(Number(n)) ? n : Number(n));
  const maxLen = Math.max(p1.length, p2.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < maxLen; i++) {
    const a = p1[i] !== undefined ? p1[i] : 0;
    const b = p2[i] !== undefined ? p2[i] : 0;
    if (typeof a === 'number' && typeof b === 'number') {
      if (a > b) return 1;
      if (a < b) return -1;
    } else {
      const cmp = String(a).localeCompare(String(b));
      if (cmp !== 0) return cmp > 0 ? 1 : -1;
    }
  }
  return 0;
}

// 递归过滤非法注入键，杜绝原型污染 (Prototype Pollution Guard)
function sanitizeObject(obj) {
  if (!obj || typeof obj !== 'object') return obj;
  if (Array.isArray(obj)) return obj.map(sanitizeObject);
  const clean = {};
  for (const k of Object.keys(obj)) {
    if (k === '__proto__' || k === 'constructor' || k === 'prototype') continue;
    clean[k] = sanitizeObject(obj[k]);
  }
  return clean;
}

// 安全合并剧情补丁：校验数据合法性，杜绝原型污染与非法注入
function _safeMergeStories(targetList, patches) {
  if (!Array.isArray(targetList) || !Array.isArray(patches)) return;
  for (let i = 0; i < patches.length; i++) {
    const p = patches[i];
    if (!p || typeof p !== 'object' || !p.id) continue;
    const cleanItem = sanitizeObject(p);
    const idx = targetList.findIndex(e => e && e.id === cleanItem.id);
    if (idx >= 0) {
      targetList[idx] = Object.assign({}, targetList[idx], cleanItem);
    } else {
      targetList.push(cleanItem);
    }
  }
}

function _safeMergeEncounters(targetList, patches) {
  if (!Array.isArray(targetList) || !Array.isArray(patches)) return;
  for (let i = 0; i < patches.length; i++) {
    const p = patches[i];
    if (!p || typeof p !== 'object' || !p.id) continue;
    const cleanItem = sanitizeObject(p);
    const idx = targetList.findIndex(e => e && e.id === cleanItem.id);
    if (idx >= 0) {
      targetList[idx] = Object.assign({}, targetList[idx], cleanItem);
    } else {
      targetList.push(cleanItem);
    }
  }
}

const HOTUPDATE_DEFAULT_SALT = 'LIFESIM_HOTUPDATE_SIGN_SALT_2026';

// 签名与哈希验算工具函数 (HMAC/Checksum Verification)
function calculateManifestChecksum(manifest, secretKey = HOTUPDATE_DEFAULT_SALT) {
  if (!manifest || typeof manifest !== 'object') return '';
  const payload = Object.keys(manifest)
    .filter(k => k !== 'signature' && k !== 'checksum' && k !== 'cloudAuthToken' && k !== 'rsaSignature')
    .sort()
    .reduce((acc, k) => {
      acc[k] = manifest[k];
      return acc;
    }, {});
  const str = JSON.stringify(payload) + ':' + (secretKey || HOTUPDATE_DEFAULT_SALT);
  let hash = 0x811c9dc5;
  for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {
    hash ^= str.charCodeAt(i);
    hash = Math.imul(hash, 0x01000193);
  }
  return (hash >>> 0).toString(16);
}

function verifyManifestSignature(manifest, secretKey = HOTUPDATE_DEFAULT_SALT, isRemote = false) {
  if (!manifest || typeof manifest !== 'object') return false;
  
  // 1. 若为云函数下发的数据包（带有合法微信云环境鉴权标识），直接受信放行
  if (manifest._fromCloudFunction === true && manifest._cloudVerified === true) {
    return true;
  }

  const expectedSig = manifest.signature || manifest.checksum || manifest.rsaSignature;
  // 2. 严防假冒与篡改：远程 HTTP/CDN 下发的热更包必须强制携带签名，绝不放行无签名包！
  if (isRemote && !expectedSig) {
    console.error('[ContentUpdater] 拒绝合并：远程热更包未携带数字签名或防伪印信！');
    return false;
  }

  // 3. 本地打包基线包在没有签名时允许加载初始底模
  if (!expectedSig) {
    return !isRemote;
  }

  // 4. 严防公开默认盐伪造：远程 HTTP/CDN 下发的热更包严禁使用源码内置的公开默认盐进行验签！
  // 必须配置专属私有动态安全密钥（secretKey !== HOTUPDATE_DEFAULT_SALT），或者通过微信云函数原生鉴权链路下发！
  if (isRemote && (!secretKey || secretKey === HOTUPDATE_DEFAULT_SALT) && !manifest.cloudAuthToken) {
    console.error('[ContentUpdater] 拒绝合并：远程热更包禁止使用源码公开默认盐进行验签！必须通过微信云函数下发或配置专属私有动态安全密钥！');
    return false;
  }

  const computed = calculateManifestChecksum(manifest, secretKey);
  return String(expectedSig).toLowerCase() === String(computed).toLowerCase();
}

// 数值安全白名单与范围限制 (Value Range Whitelist & Clamp)
function clampManifestValues(clean) {
  if (!clean || typeof clean !== 'object') return clean;

  // 1. 运营前沿线年份限制：限定在 [2026, 3100]，允许推进至深空三千年宏大视界
  if (typeof clean.frontierYear === 'number') {
    clean.frontierYear = Math.max(2026, Math.min(3100, Math.floor(clean.frontierYear)));
  }
  if (clean.worldChrono && typeof clean.worldChrono === 'object') {
    if (typeof clean.worldChrono.frontierYear === 'number') {
      clean.worldChrono.frontierYear = Math.max(2026, Math.min(3100, Math.floor(clean.worldChrono.frontierYear)));
    }
  }

  // 2. 赛道薪酬倍率限制：严格限定在 [0.50, 3.00] 之间
  if (clean.balanceOverrides && typeof clean.balanceOverrides === 'object') {
    const scale = clean.balanceOverrides.TRACK_INCOME_BONUS_SCALE;
    if (scale && typeof scale === 'object') {
      for (const k of Object.keys(scale)) {
        if (typeof scale[k] === 'number') {
          scale[k] = Math.max(0.50, Math.min(3.00, Math.round(scale[k] * 100) / 100));
        } else {
          delete scale[k];
        }
      }
    }

    // 3. 奖励与门槛限制：精准区分金钱/积分/属性，严防大额奖励与底分被误裁为 30
    if (clean.balanceOverrides.rewards && typeof clean.balanceOverrides.rewards === 'object') {
      _clampRewardsObject(clean.balanceOverrides.rewards);
    }
  }

  if (clean.rewards && typeof clean.rewards === 'object') {
    _clampRewardsObject(clean.rewards);
  }

  return clean;
}

function _clampRewardsObject(rewards) {
  for (const rk of Object.keys(rewards)) {
    if (typeof rewards[rk] === 'number') {
      // 金钱、资产与大额奖金项 [-500000, 500000]
      if (/(money|capital|gold|fund|bonus|cash|salary|wage|grant)/i.test(rk)) {
        rewards[rk] = Math.max(-500000, Math.min(500000, Math.round(rewards[rk])));
      }
      // 考核基准分与评级门槛项 [0, 50000] (如 promotionBonus, endgameSGradeBaseScore, gradeS_minScore)
      else if (/(score|threshold|base)/i.test(rk)) {
        rewards[rk] = Math.max(0, Math.min(50000, Math.round(rewards[rk])));
      }
      // 基础单次属性微调项 [-30, 30]
      else {
        rewards[rk] = Math.max(-30, Math.min(30, Math.round(rewards[rk])));
      }
    }
  }
}

function applyManifestData(manifest, targets = {}, secretKey = HOTUPDATE_DEFAULT_SALT, isRemote = false) {
  if (!manifest || typeof manifest !== 'object') return false;

  // 1. 签名与防篡改校验 (针对远程包强制要求数字签名)
  if (!verifyManifestSignature(manifest, secretKey, isRemote)) {
    console.error(`[ContentUpdater] 签名校验未通过，拒绝合并热更数据！(isRemote=${isRemote})`);
    return false;
  }

  // 2. 版本比对
  if (manifest.manifestVersion) {
    if (compareVersions(manifest.manifestVersion, _currentContentVersion) < 0) {
      console.warn(`[ContentUpdater] 忽略低版本热更包: ${manifest.manifestVersion} < 当前版本: ${_currentContentVersion}`);
      return false;
    }
    _currentContentVersion = String(manifest.manifestVersion);
  }

  // 3. 原型污染防御与数值范围白名单裁剪
  const sanitized = sanitizeObject(manifest);
  const clean = clampManifestValues(sanitized);

  // 4. 剧情补丁合并
  if (Array.isArray(clean.storiesPatch) && Array.isArray(targets.modularStories)) {
    _safeMergeStories(targets.modularStories, clean.storiesPatch);
  }

  // 5. 遭遇补丁合并
  if (Array.isArray(clean.encountersPatch) && Array.isArray(targets.encountersPool)) {
    _safeMergeEncounters(targets.encountersPool, clean.encountersPatch);
  }

  // 6. 平衡数值覆盖与赛道薪酬倍率覆盖
  if (clean.balanceOverrides && typeof clean.balanceOverrides === 'object') {
    if (targets.dynamicConfig) {
      if (clean.balanceOverrides.rewards && targets.dynamicConfig.rewards) {
        Object.assign(targets.dynamicConfig.rewards, clean.balanceOverrides.rewards);
      }
      if (clean.balanceOverrides.endingThresholds && targets.dynamicConfig.endingThresholds) {
        Object.assign(targets.dynamicConfig.endingThresholds, clean.balanceOverrides.endingThresholds);
      }
    }
    if (clean.balanceOverrides.TRACK_INCOME_BONUS_SCALE && targets.trackIncomeBonusScale) {
      Object.assign(targets.trackIncomeBonusScale, clean.balanceOverrides.TRACK_INCOME_BONUS_SCALE);
    }
  }

  // 7. 文案与提示语热更覆盖 (textOverrides)
  if (clean.textOverrides && typeof clean.textOverrides === 'object' && targets.textOverrides) {
    Object.assign(targets.textOverrides, clean.textOverrides);
  }

  // 8. 运营前沿线动态下发 (frontierYear / worldChrono)
  if (clean.frontierYear && typeof clean.frontierYear === 'number') {
    if (targets.state) targets.state.currentFrontierYear = clean.frontierYear;
    if (targets.currentFrontierYear !== undefined) targets.currentFrontierYear = clean.frontierYear;
  }
  if (clean.worldChrono && typeof clean.worldChrono === 'object') {
    if (targets.worldChrono) {
      Object.assign(targets.worldChrono, clean.worldChrono);
    }
    if (clean.worldChrono.frontierYear) {
      if (targets.state) targets.state.currentFrontierYear = clean.worldChrono.frontierYear;
    }
  }

  return true;
}

function initContentManifest(targets = {}) {
  const secretKey = (targets && targets.dynamicConfig && targets.dynamicConfig.hotUpdateSecret) || HOTUPDATE_DEFAULT_SALT;
  // 1. 优先加载本地打包的 manifest 基线 (非 remote)
  if (_localManifest) {
    applyManifestData(_localManifest, targets, secretKey, false);
  }

  // 2. 读取 Storage 缓存的高版本 manifest (本地缓存)
  try {
    let cachedStr = null;
    if (typeof wx !== 'undefined' && typeof wx.getStorageSync === 'function') {
      cachedStr = wx.getStorageSync('LIFESIM_CACHED_CONTENT_MANIFEST');
    } else if (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined') {
      cachedStr = localStorage.getItem('LIFESIM_CACHED_CONTENT_MANIFEST');
    }
    if (cachedStr) {
      const parsed = typeof cachedStr === 'string' ? JSON.parse(cachedStr) : cachedStr;
      if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object') {
        applyManifestData(parsed, targets, secretKey, false);
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn('[ContentUpdater] 读取本地缓存 manifest 异常:', e);
  }

  return _currentContentVersion;
}

function fetchRemoteContentManifest(remoteUrl, targets = {}, onUpdated = null) {
  const secretKey = (targets && targets.dynamicConfig && targets.dynamicConfig.hotUpdateSecret) || HOTUPDATE_DEFAULT_SALT;

  // 1. 优先采用微信云函数通道获取官方受信任热更包 (防 MITM 篡改、微信原生私有链路)
  if (typeof wx !== 'undefined' && wx.cloud && typeof wx.cloud.callFunction === 'function') {
    wx.cloud.callFunction({
      name: 'clanLeaderboard',
      data: { action: 'getContentManifest' }
    }).then(res => {
      if (res && res.result && res.result.success && res.result.manifest) {
        const cloudManifest = res.result.manifest;
        cloudManifest._fromCloudFunction = true;
        cloudManifest._cloudVerified = true;
        const applied = applyManifestData(cloudManifest, targets, secretKey, true);
        if (applied) {
          try {
            if (typeof wx.setStorageSync === 'function') {
              wx.setStorageSync('LIFESIM_CACHED_CONTENT_MANIFEST', JSON.stringify(cloudManifest));
            }
          } catch (_) {}
          console.log(`[ContentUpdater] 云函数通道成功拉取权威热更包版本: ${cloudManifest.manifestVersion}`);
          if (typeof onUpdated === 'function') onUpdated(cloudManifest);
          return;
        }
      }
      _fetchViaHttpCdn(remoteUrl, targets, secretKey, onUpdated);
    }).catch(err => {
      console.warn('[ContentUpdater] 云函数热更拉取失败，尝试备用 CDN 通道:', err);
      _fetchViaHttpCdn(remoteUrl, targets, secretKey, onUpdated);
    });
    return;
  }

  // 2. 外部 HTTP/CDN 备用通道拉取
  _fetchViaHttpCdn(remoteUrl, targets, secretKey, onUpdated);
}

function _fetchViaHttpCdn(remoteUrl, targets, secretKey, onUpdated) {
  if (!remoteUrl || typeof remoteUrl !== 'string' || !remoteUrl.startsWith('http')) return;
  if (typeof wx === 'undefined' || typeof wx.request !== 'function') return;

  wx.request({
    url: remoteUrl,
    method: 'GET',
    timeout: 5000,
    success: (res) => {
      if (res && res.statusCode === 200 && res.data) {
        const data = typeof res.data === 'string' ? JSON.parse(res.data) : res.data;
        if (data && typeof data === 'object' && data.manifestVersion) {
          // 远程包强制要求 isRemote: true 校验数字签名
          const applied = applyManifestData(data, targets, secretKey, true);
          if (applied) {
            try {
              if (typeof wx.setStorageSync === 'function') {
                wx.setStorageSync('LIFESIM_CACHED_CONTENT_MANIFEST', JSON.stringify(data));
              }
            } catch (_) {}
            console.log(`[ContentUpdater] CDN 渠道成功热更剧情与平衡内容至版本: ${data.manifestVersion}`);
            if (typeof onUpdated === 'function') {
              onUpdated(data);
            }
          }
        }
      }
    },
    fail: (err) => {
      console.warn('[ContentUpdater] 远程热更拉取失败，平滑沿用本地基线:', err);
    }
  });
}

module.exports = {
  getContentVersion,
  initContentManifest,
  applyManifestData,
  fetchRemoteContentManifest,
  calculateManifestChecksum,
  verifyManifestSignature,
  clampManifestValues,
  HOTUPDATE_DEFAULT_SALT
};
</file>

<file path="wechat-minigame/src/data/epochOneEngine.js">
// 华夏三千年世家大系 · 第一纪元：汉末筑基与建安风骨 (公元100~300年) 引擎逻辑
const { HAN_WEI_CRITICAL_CHRONO_EVENTS } = require('./historicalMilieuHanWei');

// 统一引用第一赛季（公元100~300年）唯一真源历史关隘大案（截取不超过公元300年）
const EPOCH_ONE_HISTORICAL_EVENTS = (HAN_WEI_CRITICAL_CHRONO_EVENTS || []).filter(e => typeof e.year === 'number' && e.year <= 300);


/**
 * 推进第一纪元执行状态
 */
function runEpochOneEvent(eventIndex, optionIndex, currentAncestorState) {
  const evt = EPOCH_ONE_HISTORICAL_EVENTS[eventIndex];
  if (!evt) return null;
  const opt = evt.options[optionIndex];
  if (!opt) return null;

  const result = {
    eventId: evt.id,
    eventTitle: evt.title,
    eventYear: evt.year,
    chosenText: opt.text,
    success: true,
    chronicleSnippet: ''
  };

  // 检定逻辑 (支持 reqStat 与 reqTrack 多维赛道映射)
  let statKey = opt.reqStat;
  if (!statKey && opt.reqTrack) {
    const tr = String(opt.reqTrack).toLowerCase();
    if (['official', 'gov', 'politics'].includes(tr)) statKey = 'rep';
    else if (['scholar', 'edu', 'study'].includes(tr)) statKey = 'iq';
    else if (['merchant', 'biz', 'trade'].includes(tr)) statKey = 'iq';
    else if (['military', 'soldier'].includes(tr)) statKey = 'courage';
    else if (['recluse', 'hermit'].includes(tr)) statKey = 'clean';
    else if (['medical', 'doctor', 'herb'].includes(tr)) statKey = 'health';
    else statKey = 'courage';
  }
  if (statKey && typeof opt.reqVal === 'number') {
    const val = currentAncestorState[statKey] !== undefined ? currentAncestorState[statKey] : (currentAncestorState.courage || 50);
    if (val < opt.reqVal) {
      result.success = false;
      const pen = opt.failPenalty || {};
      for (const k in pen) {
        if (k !== 'chronicleText' && k !== 'text') currentAncestorState[k] = (currentAncestorState[k] || 0) + pen[k];
      }
      result.chronicleSnippet = pen.chronicleText || pen.text || '经历波折，勉力支应。';
      return result;
    }
  }

  // 扣除成本
  if (opt.cost) {
    for (const k in opt.cost) {
      currentAncestorState[k] = (currentAncestorState[k] || 0) - opt.cost[k];
    }
  }

  // 获得收益
  const rew = opt.successReward || {};
  for (const k in rew) {
    if (k !== 'chronicleText' && k !== 'text' && k !== 'newMottoTag' && k !== 'officialTitle') {
      currentAncestorState[k] = (currentAncestorState[k] || 0) + rew[k];
    }
  }
  if (rew.officialTitle) {
    currentAncestorState.officialTitle = rew.officialTitle;
  }
  if (rew.newMottoTag) {
    currentAncestorState.mottoTags = currentAncestorState.mottoTags || [];
    currentAncestorState.mottoTags.push(rew.newMottoTag);
  }
  result.chronicleSnippet = rew.chronicleText || rew.text || '行止有定，族风渐清。';
  return result;
}

/**
 * 结算第一纪元终局：生成第一代始祖归档卡
 */
function settleEpochOneAncestor(familyProfile, ancestorState, decisionHistory = []) {
  const givenName = ancestorState.name || '远之';
  const ziHao = ancestorState.ziHao || '伯思';
  const official = ancestorState.officialTitle || '汉故郡功曹';
  
  // 综合评定谥号
  let posthumous = '文靖公';
  if ((ancestorState.courage || 50) > 70) posthumous = '武襄公';
  else if ((ancestorState.morality || 50) > 70) posthumous = '文肃公';
  else if ((ancestorState.iq || 50) > 70) posthumous = '文宪公';

  // 自动沉淀第一代新家训
  let derivedMotto = '居安思危，深壁固垒。事亲必孝，睦恤九族。';
  if (ancestorState.mottoTags && ancestorState.mottoTags.includes('以德化暴')) {
    derivedMotto = '天道好还，仁者无敌。逢荒必开仓，待客当推诚。';
  } else if (ancestorState.mottoTags && ancestorState.mottoTags.includes('杏林济众')) {
    derivedMotto = '精诚修仁，不务虚名。活人者寿，世守药囊。';
  }

  const deeds = decisionHistory.map(d => `【${d.eventYear}年】${d.chronicleSnippet}`);

  // 动态依据经历的历史大案计算生卒年份 (避免死硬编码与时代错位)
  const years = decisionHistory.map(d => d.eventYear).filter(y => typeof y === 'number');
  const minYear = years.length > 0 ? Math.min(...years) : 168;
  const maxYear = years.length > 0 ? Math.max(...years) : 220;
  const birthYear = ancestorState.birthYear || Math.max(100, minYear - 18);
  const deathYear = ancestorState.deathYear || Math.min(300, Math.max(birthYear + 60, maxYear));
  const lifespan = [birthYear, deathYear];

  // 依据真实抉择动态撰写始祖墓志铭
  let epitaph = '';
  if (decisionHistory.length > 0) {
    const highlights = decisionHistory.map(d => d.chronicleSnippet || d.chosenText).filter(Boolean);
    epitaph = `公生于汉末，长于世族。一生经略风云：${highlights.slice(0, 3).join('；')}。保全宗脉，德荫子孙，百代不朽。`;
  } else {
    epitaph = `生于汉魏风骨之朝，率族人立坞自保、修明经训，卒保宗族老幼无虞。后世百代，皆本于公之余荫。`;
  }

  return {
    eraIndex: 1,
    eraName: '汉魏风骨',
    givenName,
    ziHao,
    fullName: `${familyProfile.surname || ''}${givenName}`,
    lifespan,
    officialTitle: official,
    posthumousTitle: posthumous,
    epitaph,
    finalDeeds: deeds,
    bequeathedMoney: Math.max(1000, ancestorState.money || 3000),
    prestigeGain: ancestorState.clanPrestige || 60,
    newMotto: {
      era: '第一世·汉魏留训',
      text: derivedMotto,
      author: `${familyProfile.surname}公${givenName}`
    },
    passedRelic: ancestorState.relicId ? { id: ancestorState.relicId, name: '建安医圣秘本/古文经疏' } : null
  };
}

if (typeof module !== 'undefined' && module.exports) {
  module.exports = {
    EPOCH_ONE_HISTORICAL_EVENTS,
    runEpochOneEvent,
    settleEpochOneAncestor
  };
}

// 华夏三千年世家大系 · 家族建制与宗族底蕴管理系统 (FamilySystem)
// 规范：真实姓氏 + 省份 + 堂号 + 家业底色（不可更改，严格合规，脱敏防沉迷）

// 支持的百家姓精选库（亦支持自定义合法姓氏输入）

// 严禁恶意冒用名门与现代敏感人物黑名单（合规防线）
const SENSITIVE_FAMILY_KEYWORDS = [

    statBonus: { resilience: 18, techInventionBonus: 0.3, startingMoney: 5000 },

/**
 * 校验并创建家族核心档案 (不可篡改)
 */
function createFamilyProfile(paramsOrSurname, maybeProvince, maybeHall, maybeVocation, maybeMotto, maybeOpenid) {
  let surname, province, hallName, vocationId, customMotto, openid;
  if (paramsOrSurname && typeof paramsOrSurname === 'object') {
    ({ surname, province, hallName, vocationId, customMotto, openid } = paramsOrSurname);
  } else {
    surname = paramsOrSurname;
    province = maybeProvince;
    hallName = maybeHall;
    vocationId = maybeVocation;
    customMotto = maybeMotto;
    openid = maybeOpenid;
  }

  // 1. 基础字段非空与字符合法性校验（支持单姓、复姓及自定义合法姓氏）
  if (!surname || typeof surname !== 'string' || surname.trim().length === 0) {
    throw new Error('家族姓氏不能为空');
  }
  const rawSurname = surname.trim();
  if (!/^[\u4e00-\u9fa5]{1,2}$/.test(rawSurname)) {
    throw new Error('家族姓氏须为1至2位合规中文字符');
  }

  // 2. 省份校验
  if (!province || !PROVINCE_LIST.includes(province)) {
    throw new Error('必须选择合规的祖籍省份');
  }

  // 3. 堂号与敏感词校验（按整词精确拦截，严禁子串误伤常见姓氏）
  const hallStr = Array.isArray(hallName) ? (hallName[0] || `${rawSurname}氏堂`) : hallName;
  const rawHall = (hallStr && typeof hallStr === 'string' && hallStr.trim()) ? hallStr.trim() : `${rawSurname}氏堂`;
  const combined = `${rawSurname}${rawHall}`;
  for (const kw of SENSITIVE_FAMILY_KEYWORDS) {
    if (combined.includes(kw) || rawSurname === kw || rawHall === kw || rawHall.includes(kw)) {
      throw new Error('家名或堂号包含受限敏感词汇，请重新命名');
    }
  }

  const cleanSurname = rawSurname.slice(0, 2); // 规范为单姓或复姓
  const cleanHall = rawHall.slice(0, 8);

  // 4. 家业底色校验
  const vocation = VOCATION_ORIGIN_PROFILES[vocationId] || VOCATION_ORIGIN_PROFILES['study_scholar'];

  const familyProfile = {
    familyId: openid ? `fam_${openid.slice(-8)}_${cleanSurname}_${Date.now().toString(36)}` : `fam_${Date.now()}_${Math.floor(Math.random() * 10000)}`,
    openid: openid || null,
    surname: cleanSurname,
    province: province,
    hallName: cleanHall,
    fullName: `${province}·${cleanSurname}氏·${cleanHall}`,
    vocationId: vocation.id,
    vocationName: vocation.name,
    createdAt: Date.now(),
    isImmutable: true, // 核心标记：不可更改

    currentSeasonId: 'season_1_han_wei',
    // 时代推进进度 (默认始于公元100年汉末)
    timeline: {
      currentEpochIndex: 1,
      currentYear: 100,
      isWaitingFrontier: false,
      completedEpochs: {}
    },

    // 宗族公共底蕴池 (跨越三千年永久继承)
    clanTrust: {
      trustFundMoney: vocation.statBonus.startingMoney || 5000,
      prestigeScore: 50,
      ancestralTempleTier: 1, // 1级草庐宗茔 -> 9级名刹大观
      accumulatedMerit: 0,
      mottoList: [
        {
          era: '汉末立族',
          text: customMotto && customMotto.trim() ? customMotto.trim().slice(0, 30) : vocation.initialMotto,
          effectDesc: '初代始祖立世誓诫，万代尊奉'
        }
      ],
      relics: [] // 传世重宝信物
    },

    // 族谱世系长卷 (不可篡改的墨印档案)
    genealogyList: [],

    // 社交同盟网络
    alliances: []
  };

  return familyProfile;
}

/**
 * 载入一位先祖功业至族谱长卷 (幂等防刷写入)
 */
function recordAncestorToGenealogy(family, ancestorRecord) {
  if (!family || !family.genealogyList) return false;

  const isGenerational = !!ancestorRecord.isGenerationalRun;
  const targetEra = ancestorRecord.eraIndex !== undefined ? ancestorRecord.eraIndex : (family.genealogyList.length + 1);

  // 查重：若是历朝纪元推演，按 eraIndex 幂等去重；若是代际亲历局，按 recordId 幂等去重
  const existingIdx = isGenerational
    ? (ancestorRecord.recordId ? family.genealogyList.findIndex(a => a.recordId === ancestorRecord.recordId) : -1)
    : family.genealogyList.findIndex(a => a.eraIndex === targetEra && !a.isGenerationalRun);

  if (existingIdx >= 0) {
    // 幂等更新：若该条目已归档，仅刷新谥号与勋业，绝不重复虚增代数、族产与声望
    const existing = family.genealogyList[existingIdx];
    if (ancestorRecord.fullName) existing.fullName = ancestorRecord.fullName;
    else if (ancestorRecord.givenName) existing.fullName = `${family.surname}${ancestorRecord.givenName}`;
    if (ancestorRecord.ziHao) existing.ziHao = ancestorRecord.ziHao;
    if (ancestorRecord.officialTitle) existing.officialTitle = ancestorRecord.officialTitle;
    if (ancestorRecord.epitaph) existing.epitaph = ancestorRecord.epitaph;
    if (ancestorRecord.finalDeeds && ancestorRecord.finalDeeds.length > 0) existing.finalDeeds = ancestorRecord.finalDeeds;
    if (ancestorRecord.successorOrigin) existing.successorOrigin = ancestorRecord.successorOrigin;
    if (ancestorRecord.successorRole) existing.successorRole = ancestorRecord.successorRole;
    return existing;
  }

  const entry = {
    recordId: ancestorRecord.recordId || `rec_${Date.now()}_${Math.floor(Math.random() * 10000)}`,
    isGenerationalRun: isGenerational,
    generation: ancestorRecord.generation || (family.genealogyList.length + 1),
    eraIndex: targetEra,
    eraName: ancestorRecord.eraName || (isGenerational ? `当代承续` : '汉末筑基'),
    successorOrigin: ancestorRecord.successorOrigin || 'lineage_child',
    successorRole: ancestorRecord.successorRole || '宗门嫡长',
    fullName: ancestorRecord.fullName || `${family.surname}${ancestorRecord.givenName || '先祖'}`,
    ziHao: ancestorRecord.ziHao || '德远',
    lifespan: ancestorRecord.lifespan || [160, 220],
    officialTitle: ancestorRecord.officialTitle || '布衣名士',
    posthumousTitle: ancestorRecord.posthumousTitle || '仁靖公',
    epitaph: ancestorRecord.epitaph || '躬耕保族，虽经兵燹，文脉未坠。',
    finalDeeds: ancestorRecord.finalDeeds || [],
    passedRelic: ancestorRecord.passedRelic || null,
    recordedTimestamp: Date.now()
  };

  family.genealogyList.push(entry);

  // 宗族资产沉淀 (首次归档时发放)
  if (ancestorRecord.bequeathedMoney) {
    family.clanTrust.trustFundMoney += Math.floor(ancestorRecord.bequeathedMoney * 0.3); // 30% 归宗族公产
  }
  if (ancestorRecord.prestigeGain) {
    family.clanTrust.prestigeScore += ancestorRecord.prestigeGain;
  }
  if (ancestorRecord.newMotto) {
    family.clanTrust.mottoList.push(ancestorRecord.newMotto);
  }
  if (ancestorRecord.passedRelic) {
    family.clanTrust.relics.push(ancestorRecord.passedRelic);
  }

  if (family.timeline) {
    family.timeline.completedEpochs = family.timeline.completedEpochs || {};
    family.timeline.completedEpochs[targetEra] = true;
    family.timeline.currentEpochIndex = Math.min(15, Math.max(family.timeline.currentEpochIndex || 1, targetEra + 1));
  }

  return entry;
}

function upgradeAncestralTemple(family, paymentSource) {
  if (!family || !family.clanTrust) return { success: false, reason: '未确立世家档案' };
  const curTier = family.clanTrust.ancestralTempleTier || 1;
  if (curTier >= 9) return { success: false, reason: '宗庙已达至尊万世华夏宗庙(9阶)！' };
  const targetSpec = ANCESTRAL_TEMPLE_TIERS[curTier]; // next tier index is curTier
  const cost = targetSpec.upgradeCost;

  if (paymentSource === 'capital') {
    // handled by caller
  } else {
    const curTrustFund = family.clanTrust.trustFundMoney || 0;
    if (curTrustFund < cost) {
      return { success: false, reason: `族产公积不足，需公积金 ${cost.toLocaleString()} 五铢钱` };
    }
    family.clanTrust.trustFundMoney -= cost;
  }

  family.clanTrust.ancestralTempleTier = curTier + 1;
  const prestigeGain = targetSpec.tier * 30;
  family.clanTrust.prestigeScore = (family.clanTrust.prestigeScore || 50) + prestigeGain;

  return {
    success: true,
    newTier: targetSpec.tier,
    tierName: targetSpec.name,
    prestigeGain: prestigeGain,
    perk: targetSpec.perk
  };
}

function calculateClanTrustAnnualDividend(family) {
  if (!family || !family.clanTrust) return 0;
  const tier = family.clanTrust.ancestralTempleTier || 1;
  const fund = family.clanTrust.trustFundMoney || 5000;
  const rate = 0.04 + tier * 0.01;
  return Math.max(1000, Math.round(fund * rate));
}

function addClanAlliance(family, allianceHouse, currentYear) {
  if (!family || !family.alliances) return { success: false, reason: '世家档案未就绪' };
  family.alliances = family.alliances || [];
  const existing = family.alliances.find(a => a.surname === allianceHouse.surname);
  if (existing) {
    return { success: false, reason: `我族与【${allianceHouse.province}·${allianceHouse.surname}氏】早已缔结世交盟约！` };
  }
  const bond = {
    surname: allianceHouse.surname,
    province: allianceHouse.province,
    hallName: allianceHouse.hallName,
    desc: allianceHouse.desc,
    bondYear: currentYear || 2026,
    prestigeBonus: allianceHouse.prestigeBonus || 45
  };
  family.alliances.push(bond);
  family.clanTrust.trustFundMoney = (family.clanTrust.trustFundMoney || 5000) + (allianceHouse.dowryGift || 8000);
  family.clanTrust.prestigeScore = (family.clanTrust.prestigeScore || 50) + (allianceHouse.prestigeBonus || 45);

  return { success: true, bond: bond };
}

function preResearchSeasonDoctrine(family, nextSeasonId, doctrineName) {
  if (!family || !family.clanTrust) return { success: false, reason: '世家档案未就绪' };
  family.clanTrust.preResearchedSeasons = family.clanTrust.preResearchedSeasons || {};
  if (family.clanTrust.preResearchedSeasons[nextSeasonId]) {
    return { success: false, reason: `我族已精研领悟【${doctrineName}】之先祖要旨！` };
  }
  const curScore = family.clanTrust.prestigeScore || 0;
  if (curScore < 30) {
    return { success: false, reason: '需宗族功德声望达到 30 分方可研读经世密卷！' };
  }
  family.clanTrust.preResearchedSeasons[nextSeasonId] = {
    doctrineName: doctrineName,
    researchedAt: Date.now()
  };
  return { success: true, doctrineName: doctrineName };
}

if (typeof module !== 'undefined' && module.exports) {
  module.exports = {
    COMMON_SURNAMES,
    PROVINCE_LIST,
    FAMOUS_HALL_MAPPING,
    VOCATION_ORIGIN_PROFILES,
    ANCESTRAL_TEMPLE_TIERS,
    HISTORICAL_CLAN_HOUSES,
    createFamilyProfile,
    recordAncestorToGenealogy,
    upgradeAncestralTemple,
    calculateClanTrustAnnualDividend,
    addClanAlliance,
    preResearchSeasonDoctrine
  };
}

// 华夏三千年世家大系 · 未来推演前沿引擎 (FutureFrontierEngine)
// 全系时间轴：公元 100 年 ～ 3100 年（整整3000年跨度）
// 第一阶段研发视界：公元 2100 年；第二阶段百年深空纪元：公元 2100 ～ 3100 年
// 【特别说明】：未来推演场景均属于基于硬核前沿科技发展的宏观科幻沙盒推演，文中所涉机构、工程与事件皆为艺术化架空设计，绝不代表真实机构或现实既成事实。

// 全服世界时钟常量 (支持服务端配置与动态计算)
const GLOBAL_WORLD_CHRONO = {
  systemTitle: '华夏三千年世家大系',
  startYear: 100,             // 公元100年 (东汉末年筑基)
  endYear: 3100,               // 公元3100年 (三千载宏观终局)
  spanYears: 3000,             // 跨度 3000 年 (公元100~3100年)
  yearsPerGeneration: 50,      // 统一标准：平均50年一世，三千年约60世

  // 两大阶段战略视界划分
  phases: {
    phase1: {
      id: 'phase1_historic_and_near_future',
      name: '筑基至近未来视界',
      startYear: 100,
      horizonYear: 2100,
      spanYears: 2000,
      desc: '第一阶段视界（公元100~2100年）：两千年风云筑基与近未来星汉初开'
    },
    phase2: {
      id: 'phase2_deep_space_centennial',
      name: '深空与星汉未来纪元',
      startYear: 2100,
      horizonYear: 3100,
      spanYears: 1000,
      desc: '第二阶段百年纪元（公元2100~3100年）：地月系、内太阳系与柯伊伯带深空世家探索'
    }
  },

  // 当前客户端与内容研发的第一阶段视界（公元2100年）
  horizonYear: 2100,
  // 运营当前实际开放的前沿线年份（开服设为2035，随运营热更推进）
  defaultFrontierYear: 2035
};

/**
 * 根据年份动态计算传承世数 (世数 = 历经年数 / 每世年数)
 */
function calculateGenerationCount(startYear = GLOBAL_WORLD_CHRONO.startYear, targetYear = 2026, yearsPerGen = GLOBAL_WORLD_CHRONO.yearsPerGeneration) {
  const elapsed = Math.max(0, targetYear - startYear);
  return Math.max(1, Math.round(elapsed / yearsPerGen));
}

/**
 * 动态生成精准年数的前沿提示语，消除硬编码文案偏差
 */
function getFrontierBarrierPrompt(currentYear = 2026, frontierYear = GLOBAL_WORLD_CHRONO.defaultFrontierYear) {
  const elapsedYears = Math.max(0, currentYear - GLOBAL_WORLD_CHRONO.startYear);
  const remainingYears = Math.max(0, GLOBAL_WORLD_CHRONO.endYear - currentYear);
  return {
    title: '岁月未至 · 天机尚隐',
    quote: `${elapsedYears}载风云皆成过往，${remainingYears}年星汉浩瀚待开。先祖垂眸凝视，且待下一个时代风云际会……`,
    subText: '本世家已昂首迈至时代前沿！请前往【宗祠大院】进香祭祖、打理家族信托、或与同盟世家联姻。',
    elapsedYears,
    remainingYears,
    frontierYear
  };
}

        successReward: { clanPrestige: 980, courage: 150, posthumousTitle: '星汉远征公', chronicleText: '三千载不过一瞬，更辽远的深空才是终极归宿！宗族旗舰再度拔锚启航！' },
        failPenalty: { courage: -10, chronicleText: '星际拔锚远航雄关漫道，旗舰承载族人希望启程。' }
      }
    ]
  }
];

/**
 * 校验世家是否可以推进到下一未来年代
 */
function checkFrontierAccessibility(familyProfileOrYear, currentFrontierYear = GLOBAL_WORLD_CHRONO.defaultFrontierYear) {
  const currentYear = typeof familyProfileOrYear === 'number'
    ? familyProfileOrYear
    : (familyProfileOrYear && familyProfileOrYear.timeline ? familyProfileOrYear.timeline.currentYear : 2026);

  // 1. 达到三千年终局 3100 年
  if (currentYear >= GLOBAL_WORLD_CHRONO.endYear) {
    return {
      canAdvance: false,
      reason: 'HORIZON_REACHED',
      message: '已达到世家大系之【三千载大成终局】（公元3100年）！三十个世纪波澜壮阔，华夏百家姓谱系已成为银河永恒丰碑！'
    };
  }

  // 若当前运营开放线未超过第一阶段视界（<=2100），达到2100即达成第一阶段巅峰视界
  if (currentFrontierYear <= GLOBAL_WORLD_CHRONO.horizonYear && currentYear >= GLOBAL_WORLD_CHRONO.horizonYear) {
    return {
      canAdvance: false,
      reason: 'HORIZON_REACHED',
      message: '已达到世家大系之【第一阶段视界】（公元2100年）！第二阶段深空与星汉百年纪元（公元2100~3100年）即将随运营开放！感谢您见证这漫长波澜的世家史诗！'
    };
  }

  // 2. 超过当前运营开放前沿线
  if (currentYear >= currentFrontierYear) {
    const prompt = getFrontierBarrierPrompt(currentYear, currentFrontierYear);
    return {
      canAdvance: false,
      reason: 'FRONTIER_BLOCKED',
      frontierYear: currentFrontierYear,
      barrierPrompt: prompt,
      message: `岁月未至（当前前沿：${currentFrontierYear}年）。请稍待运营开放下一个时代！`
    };
  }

  return {
    canAdvance: true,
    currentYear,
    frontierYear: currentFrontierYear
  };
}

/**
 * 执行指定年份的未来事件 (支持先祖体系与现代人生体系属性双向同步，且防止数值负溢出)
 */
function runFutureDecadeEvent(yearOrIndex, optionIndex, ancestorState) {
  const scenario = (typeof yearOrIndex === 'number' && yearOrIndex < 100)
    ? FUTURE_DECADE_SCENARIOS[yearOrIndex]
    : FUTURE_DECADE_SCENARIOS.find(s => s.year === yearOrIndex);
  if (!scenario) return null;
  const opt = scenario.options[optionIndex];
  if (!opt) return null;

  // 1. 属性与职业线检定判定
  let statKey = opt.reqStat;
  if (!statKey && opt.reqTrack) {
    const tr = String(opt.reqTrack).toLowerCase();
    if (['official', 'gov'].includes(tr)) statKey = 'rep';
    else if (['scholar', 'edu', 'tech'].includes(tr)) statKey = 'iq';
    else if (['merchant', 'biz', 'trade'].includes(tr)) statKey = 'iq';
    else if (['military', 'soldier'].includes(tr)) statKey = 'courage';
    else if (['recluse', 'med'].includes(tr)) statKey = 'clean';
    else statKey = 'courage';
  }
  if (statKey && typeof opt.reqVal === 'number') {
    const curVal = ancestorState[statKey] !== undefined ? ancestorState[statKey] : (ancestorState.courage || 50);
    if (curVal < opt.reqVal) {
      const pen = opt.failPenalty || {};
      for (const k in pen) {
        if (k !== 'chronicleText' && k !== 'text') {
          ancestorState[k] = (ancestorState[k] || 0) + pen[k];
        }
      }
      return {
        year: scenario.year,
        title: scenario.title,
        chosenText: opt.text,
        success: false,
        chronicleSnippet: pen.chronicleText || pen.text || '深空探险遭逢险阻，经受严峻考验。',
        macroWeight: {}
      };
    }
  }

  // 2. 校验并扣减成本 (杜绝族产扣成负数)
  if (opt.cost) {
    for (const k in opt.cost) {
      const c = opt.cost[k] || 0;
      if (k === 'money' || k === 'capital') {
        const cur = ancestorState.money !== undefined ? ancestorState.money : (ancestorState.capital || 0);
        const nextVal = Math.max(0, cur - c);
        ancestorState.money = nextVal;
        ancestorState.capital = nextVal;
      } else {
        ancestorState[k] = Math.max(0, (ancestorState[k] || 0) - c);
      }
    }
  }

  // 2. 发放奖励并同步双重属性体系 (先祖体系与当代人生体系无缝映射)
  const rew = opt.successReward || {};
  for (const k in rew) {
    if (k !== 'chronicleText' && k !== 'officialTitle' && k !== 'relicId') {
      const val = rew[k];
      if (k === 'money') {
        ancestorState.money = (ancestorState.money || 0) + val;
        ancestorState.capital = (ancestorState.capital || 0) + val;
      } else if (k === 'morality') {
        ancestorState.morality = (ancestorState.morality || 0) + val;
        ancestorState.clean = Math.min(100, (ancestorState.clean || 50) + Math.min(20, Math.floor(val * 0.3)));
      } else if (k === 'clanPrestige') {
        ancestorState.clanPrestige = (ancestorState.clanPrestige || 0) + val;
        ancestorState.rep = Math.min(100, (ancestorState.rep || 50) + Math.min(20, Math.floor(val * 0.2)));
      } else {
        ancestorState[k] = (ancestorState[k] || 0) + val;
      }
    }
  }
  if (rew.officialTitle) ancestorState.officialTitle = rew.officialTitle;
  if (rew.relicId) ancestorState.relicId = rew.relicId;

  return {
    year: scenario.year,
    title: scenario.title,
    chosenText: opt.text,
    chronicleSnippet: rew.chronicleText || '开创新路，砥砺前行。',
    macroWeight: opt.macroWeight || {}
  };
}

/**
 * 推进家族未来十年重大抉择 (接入 checkFrontierAccessibility 拦截与信托基金结算)
 */
function executeFutureDecadeDecision(familyProfile, targetYear, optionIndex, currentFrontierYear) {
  if (!familyProfile) throw new Error('家族档案不存在');
  const frontierLimit = currentFrontierYear || GLOBAL_WORLD_CHRONO.defaultFrontierYear;

  // 校验前沿线准入
  const access = checkFrontierAccessibility(targetYear - 10, frontierLimit);
  if (!access.canAdvance && targetYear > frontierLimit) {
    throw new Error(access.message || `岁月未至（当前前沿：${frontierLimit}年）。请稍待运营开放下一个时代！`);
  }

  const scenario = FUTURE_DECADE_SCENARIOS.find(s => s.year === targetYear);
  if (!scenario) throw new Error(`未找到公元 ${targetYear} 年的时代重大转折节点`);
  const opt = scenario.options[optionIndex] || scenario.options[0];

  // 扣减族产成本 (校验族产是否充足，并支持以宗族声望授信抵扣部分资财门槛)
  const costMoney = opt.cost ? (opt.cost.money || 0) : 0;
  const currentTrustMoney = familyProfile.clanTrust ? (familyProfile.clanTrust.trustFundMoney || 0) : 0;
  const prestigeScore = (familyProfile.clanTrust && familyProfile.clanTrust.prestigeScore) || 50;
  // 资财门槛平滑机制：当宗族具备声望时，可获得声誉背书抵扣（最高冲抵 50% 成本，且不低于 0）
  const prestigeOffset = Math.min(Math.floor(costMoney * 0.5), Math.max(0, prestigeScore - 25) * 150);
  const requiredTrustMoney = Math.max(0, costMoney - prestigeOffset);

  if (requiredTrustMoney > 0 && currentTrustMoney < requiredTrustMoney) {
    throw new Error(`家族信托公积金不足（需准备金 ¥${requiredTrustMoney.toLocaleString()}，现有 ¥${currentTrustMoney.toLocaleString()}；声望已授信抵扣 ¥${prestigeOffset.toLocaleString()}）。建议选择产学研轻资本合作，或通过同盟世交与前代结卷积累族产！`);
  }

  if (requiredTrustMoney > 0 && familyProfile.clanTrust) {
    familyProfile.clanTrust.trustFundMoney -= requiredTrustMoney;
  }

  // 发放回报
  const rew = opt.successReward || {};
  const gainPrestige = rew.clanPrestige || 50;
  const gainMoney = rew.money ? Math.round(rew.money * 0.1) : 0; // 10% 充实家族永续公产
  if (familyProfile.clanTrust) {
    familyProfile.clanTrust.prestigeScore = (familyProfile.clanTrust.prestigeScore || 50) + gainPrestige;
    familyProfile.clanTrust.trustFundMoney = (familyProfile.clanTrust.trustFundMoney || 0) + gainMoney;
    if (rew.relicId) {
      familyProfile.clanTrust.relics = familyProfile.clanTrust.relics || [];
      if (!familyProfile.clanTrust.relics.includes(rew.relicId)) {
        familyProfile.clanTrust.relics.push(rew.relicId);
      }
    }
  }

  // 推进年份与记录决策
  familyProfile.timeline = familyProfile.timeline || {};
  familyProfile.timeline.currentYear = targetYear;
  familyProfile.timeline.decisions = familyProfile.timeline.decisions || [];

  const decisionEntry = {
    year: targetYear,
    scenarioTitle: scenario.title,
    chosenText: opt.text,
    chronicleSnippet: rew.chronicleText || '开创新路，砥砺前行。',
    macroWeight: opt.macroWeight || {}
  };
  familyProfile.timeline.decisions.push(decisionEntry);

  return {
    success: true,
    targetYear,
    scenarioTitle: scenario.title,
    chosenText: opt.text,
    chronicleSnippet: decisionEntry.chronicleSnippet,
    effects: {
      clanPrestige: gainPrestige,
      trustFundMoney: gainMoney
    }
  };
}

/**
 * 汇总全服玩家选择，驱动下一个十年的宏观指数
 */
function aggregateServerMacroTrends(allDecisions) {
  const trends = {
    highTech: 1.0,
    bioHealth: 1.0,
    cleanEnergy: 1.0,
    deepOcean: 1.0,
    spaceIndustry: 1.0,
    ecology: 1.0
  };

  if (!Array.isArray(allDecisions) || allDecisions.length === 0) return trends;

  allDecisions.forEach(d => {
    if (!d.macroWeight) return;
    for (const k in d.macroWeight) {
      trends[k] = (trends[k] || 1.0) + (d.macroWeight[k] * 0.01);
    }
  });

  return trends;
}

if (typeof module !== 'undefined' && module.exports) {
  module.exports = {
    GLOBAL_WORLD_CHRONO,
    calculateGenerationCount,
    getFrontierBarrierPrompt,
    FUTURE_DECADE_SCENARIOS,
    checkFrontierAccessibility,
    runFutureDecadeEvent,
    executeFutureDecadeDecision,
    aggregateServerMacroTrends
  };
}

  SEASON_HISTORICAL_EVENTS,

  getSeasonEvents,
  getSeasonRankTitle
} = require('./seasonContentPacks');

// 统一将 15 赛季的职业线梯级 (ladder) 与重大历史关隘大案 (events) 注入 SEASONS_REGISTRY
SEASONS_REGISTRY.forEach(s => {
  const ladders = getSeasonCareerLadders(s.seasonIndex);
  if (ladders && Array.isArray(s.careerTracks)) {
    s.careerTracks.forEach(t => {
      t.ladder = ladders[t.id] || ladders.official || [];
    });
  }
  s.careerLadders = ladders;
  s.historicalEvents = getSeasonEvents(s.seasonIndex);
  s.events = s.historicalEvents;
});

function getSeasonById(seasonId) {
  return SEASONS_REGISTRY.find(s => s.id === seasonId || (s.aliases && s.aliases.includes(seasonId))) || SEASONS_REGISTRY[0];
}

function getSeasonByIndex(idx) {
  return SEASONS_REGISTRY.find(s => s.seasonIndex === idx) || SEASONS_REGISTRY[0];
}

function getSeasonByYear(year) {
  const y = Number(year) || 100;
  return SEASONS_REGISTRY.find(s => y >= s.startYear && y < s.endYear) || SEASONS_REGISTRY[SEASONS_REGISTRY.length - 1];
}

function isYearInSeason(year, seasonId) {
  const s = getSeasonById(seasonId);
  return year >= s.startYear && year <= s.endYear;
}

  return REAL_WORLD_SEASONS_REGISTRY.find(s => s.realSeasonId === realSeasonId) || REAL_WORLD_SEASONS_REGISTRY[0];
}

function getRealSeasonByIndex(idx) {
  return REAL_WORLD_SEASONS_REGISTRY.find(s => s.realSeasonIndex === idx) || REAL_WORLD_SEASONS_REGISTRY[0];
}

function mapWorldSeasonToRealSeason(worldSeasonId) {
  return REAL_WORLD_SEASONS_REGISTRY.find(s => s.worldSeasonId === worldSeasonId) || REAL_WORLD_SEASONS_REGISTRY[0];
}

function getRealSeasonByDate(dateOrTs) {
  const ts = typeof dateOrTs === 'string' ? new Date(dateOrTs).getTime() : Number(dateOrTs);
  if (!ts || isNaN(ts) || !Array.isArray(REAL_WORLD_SEASONS_REGISTRY) || REAL_WORLD_SEASONS_REGISTRY.length === 0) {
    return REAL_WORLD_SEASONS_REGISTRY[0];
  }

  // 若早于创世第1赛季开始时间，归入第1赛季
  const firstStart = new Date(REAL_WORLD_SEASONS_REGISTRY[0].startDate).getTime();
  if (ts < firstStart) return REAL_WORLD_SEASONS_REGISTRY[0];

  // 若晚于最终赛季结束时间，归入最新终局赛季
  const lastEnd = new Date(REAL_WORLD_SEASONS_REGISTRY[REAL_WORLD_SEASONS_REGISTRY.length - 1].endDate).getTime();
  if (ts >= lastEnd) return REAL_WORLD_SEASONS_REGISTRY[REAL_WORLD_SEASONS_REGISTRY.length - 1];

  for (let i = 0; i < REAL_WORLD_SEASONS_REGISTRY.length; i++) {
    const s = REAL_WORLD_SEASONS_REGISTRY[i];
    const start = new Date(s.startDate).getTime();
    const end = new Date(s.endDate).getTime();
    if (ts >= start && ts <= end) return s;

    // 若处于本赛季结束与下一赛季开赛间的短暂休整过渡期，归入当前休赛期的前置赛季
    if (i < REAL_WORLD_SEASONS_REGISTRY.length - 1) {
      const nextStart = new Date(REAL_WORLD_SEASONS_REGISTRY[i + 1].startDate).getTime();
      if (ts > end && ts < nextStart) {
        return s;
      }
    }
  }
  return REAL_WORLD_SEASONS_REGISTRY[0];
}

/**
 * 计算目标真实赛季的剩余倒计时
 * @param {Object} realSeason
 * @param {number|Date} [currentTs]
 * @returns {{ days: number, hours: number, minutes: number, isExpired: boolean, text: string }}
 */
function calculateSeasonCountdown(realSeason, currentTs) {
  if (!realSeason || !realSeason.endDate) {
    return { days: 0, hours: 0, minutes: 0, isExpired: true, text: '赛季已结束' };
  }
  const now = (currentTs !== undefined && currentTs !== null)
    ? (typeof currentTs === 'string' ? new Date(currentTs).getTime() : Number(currentTs))
    : Date.now();
  const end = new Date(realSeason.endDate).getTime();
  const diff = end - now;
  if (diff <= 0) {
    return { days: 0, hours: 0, minutes: 0, isExpired: true, text: '赛季已结卷' };
  }
  const days = Math.floor(diff / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
  const hours = Math.floor((diff % (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)) / (1000 * 60 * 60));
  const minutes = Math.floor((diff % (1000 * 60 * 60)) / (1000 * 60));
  return {
    days,
    hours,
    minutes,
    isExpired: false,
    text: `剩余 ${days} 天 ${hours} 小时`
  };
}


/**
 * 最小赛季内容包标准规格 (Minimum Season Content Pack - MSCP)
 * 供策划、数值与内容产线对齐，标准周期 7 周一季
 */
const SEASON_CONTENT_SPEC = {
  version: '1.0.0',
  description: '标准200年世家大系赛季内容包最小准入标准 (MSCP)',
  cycleDurationWeeks: 7,
  requirements: {
    // 1. 官制与阶梯：约 5 条主线阶梯（文臣/学士/商贾/武弁/隐逸），每条梯级 5 阶
    careerLadders: { minTracks: 5, minRanksPerTrack: 5, targetTotal: 25 },
    // 2. 纪元名宿与对话通讯录：6 ~ 10 位历史真实名仕
    historicalFigures: { minFigures: 6, maxFigures: 10, minDialoguesPerFigure: 2 },
    // 3. 历史关隘大案事件：4 ~ 12 个重大历史抉择事件（含检定、代际影响、谥号/门风标记）
    criticalChronoEvents: { minEvents: 4, maxEvents: 12, minOptionsPerEvent: 2 },
    // 4. 传世物与祖训物：2 ~ 3 件纪元特色传世重宝信物（具备长线专属被动加成）
    relicsAndHeritage: { minRelics: 2, maxRelics: 3 },
    // 5. 纪元基础池：出生地郡望池 (>=6)、父辈职业池 (>=5)、抓周物池 (>=6)
    foundationPools: { minProvinces: 6, minParentVocations: 5, minZhuazhouItems: 6 }
  }
};

  { week: 'T-0', phase: '正式开季上线', deliverables: '全服无缝切换赛季、跨季大结卷资产结转、新榜单启程' }
];

// 已发布并就绪的赛季内容包清单（第1季汉魏风骨、第2季六朝烟雨）
const READY_SEASON_CONTENT_PACKS = new Set([
  'season_1_han_wei',
  'season_2_liu_chao'
]);

/**
 * 校验目标赛季是否已研制完成并具备内容包
 * @param {string|number} seasonOrEpochId 赛季ID或纪元编号
 * @returns {boolean}
 */
function hasSeasonContentPack(seasonOrEpochId) {
  if (!seasonOrEpochId) return false;
  const sStr = String(seasonOrEpochId);
  if (READY_SEASON_CONTENT_PACKS.has(sStr)) return true;
  if (sStr === '1' || sStr === 'epoch_1' || sStr === 'RS_2026_Q4_HANWEI') return true;
  if (sStr === '2' || sStr === 'epoch_2' || sStr === 'RS_2026_WINTER_LIUCHAO') return true;
  return false;
}

    getSeasonRankTitle,
    getSeasonCareerLadders,
    getSeasonEvents,
    SEASON_CAREER_LADDERS,
    SEASON_HISTORICAL_EVENTS
  };
}

// =========================================================================
// 3. 核心 API 辅助函数
// =========================================================================

/**
 * 获取指定赛季的 5 大职业阶梯
 * @param {number} seasonIndex 1~15
 */
function getSeasonCareerLadders(seasonIndex) {
  const idx = Math.min(15, Math.max(1, Number(seasonIndex) || 1));
  return SEASON_CAREER_LADDERS[idx] || SEASON_CAREER_LADDERS[1];
}

/**
 * 获取指定赛季的历史大案列表（默认 4 大案）
 * @param {number} seasonIndex 1~15
 */
function getSeasonEvents(seasonIndex) {
  const idx = Math.min(15, Math.max(1, Number(seasonIndex) || 1));
  return SEASON_HISTORICAL_EVENTS[idx] || SEASON_HISTORICAL_EVENTS[1];
}

/**
 * 根据年份和赛道动态获取精准官阶职称对象
 * @param {string} track 赛道名称
 * @param {number} rankIndex 官阶索引 (0~5)
 * @param {number} year 历史年份 (100~3100)
 */
function getSeasonRankTitle(track, rankIndex, year) {
  const y = Number(year) || 100;
  const sIdx = Math.min(15, Math.max(1, Math.floor((y - 100) / 200) + 1));
  const ladders = getSeasonCareerLadders(sIdx);
  if (!ladders) return null;

  let trKey = 'official';
  const tStr = String(track || '').toLowerCase();
  if (['merchant', 'biz', 'trade', 'finance'].includes(tStr)) trKey = 'merchant';
  else if (['military', 'army', 'soldier'].includes(tStr)) trKey = 'military';
  else if (['scholar', 'edu', 'tech', 'study'].includes(tStr)) trKey = 'scholar';
  else if (['recluse', 'med', 'medical', 'herb', 'grassroots'].includes(tStr)) trKey = 'recluse';
  else trKey = 'official';

  const list = ladders[trKey] || ladders.official || [];
  if (!Array.isArray(list) || list.length === 0) return null;
  const rIdx = Math.min(list.length - 1, Math.max(0, Number(rankIndex) || 0));
  return list[rIdx] || list[0];
}

module.exports = {
  SEASON_CAREER_LADDERS,
  SEASON_HISTORICAL_EVENTS,
  getSeasonCareerLadders,
  getSeasonEvents,
  getSeasonRankTitle
};

<file path="wechat-minigame/src/data/seasonSettlementEngine.js">
/**
 * 华夏世家大系 · 200年赛季大结算引擎 (Season Settlement Engine)
 * 职责：
 * 1. 考定200年全族历经数代人拼搏后的门阀品阶 (寒微 / 郡中名士 / 郡望著姓 / 顶级阀阅)
 * 2. 封存本赛季族谱卷轴与先祖牌位殿堂
 * 3. 打包生成跨入下一个 200 年赛季的丰厚继承资产 (传世重宝、祖荫信托、血脉家风、下赛季阶层特权)
 */

const { SEASONS_REGISTRY, getSeasonById } = require('./seasonConfig.js');

const CLAN_NOBLE_TIERS = [
  {
    tier: 1,
    name: '寒微小姓',
    minPrestige: 0,
    titleDesc: '虽立宗祠，但门第尚微，仅保祖茔田舍，有待后世发扬。',
    seasonInheritBonus: { capitalRate: 0.35, startingStatus: 'commoner' }
  },
  {
    tier: 2,
    name: '郡中名士',
    minPrestige: 400,
    titleDesc: '代有清名，乡闾推重。历任郡守门吏与孝廉，宗族初具声势。',
    seasonInheritBonus: { capitalRate: 0.50, startingStatus: 'local_gentry' }
  },
  {
    tier: 3,
    name: '郡望著姓',
    minPrestige: 1000,
    titleDesc: '累世经学或巨商豪族，九品中正列为上品，享誉一州，名动朝野！',
    seasonInheritBonus: { capitalRate: 0.65, startingStatus: 'prominent_clan' }
  },
  {
    tier: 4,
    name: '顶级阀阅',
    minPrestige: 2500,
    titleDesc: '出将入相，与国休戚！门生故吏遍九州，鼎盛簪缨，万古不朽！',
    seasonInheritBonus: { capitalRate: 0.80, startingStatus: 'high_aristocracy' }
  }
];

function evaluateClanNobleTier(prestigeScore) {
  const score = prestigeScore || 0;
  for (let i = CLAN_NOBLE_TIERS.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    if (score >= CLAN_NOBLE_TIERS[i].minPrestige) {
      return CLAN_NOBLE_TIERS[i];
    }
  }
  return CLAN_NOBLE_TIERS[0];
}

/**
 * 考定 200 年赛季继承预览（纯读取，不产生任何副作用）
 */
function previewSeasonSettlement(familyProfile, seasonId = 'season_1_han_wei') {
  if (!familyProfile) throw new Error('家族档案不能为空');

  const currentSeason = getSeasonById(seasonId);
  const clanTrust = familyProfile.clanTrust || { trustFundMoney: 10000, prestigeScore: 50, relics: [], mottoList: [] };
  const genealogy = familyProfile.genealogyList || [];

  // 1. 考定门阀品级
  const nobleTier = evaluateClanNobleTier(clanTrust.prestigeScore);

  // 2. 统计本赛季历代先祖功绩长卷
  const ancestorsSummary = genealogy.map(a => ({
    generation: a.generation,
    fullName: a.fullName,
    posthumousTitle: a.posthumousTitle,
    officialTitle: a.officialTitle,
    lifespan: a.lifespan,
    epitaph: a.epitaph,
    successorOrigin: a.successorOrigin || 'lineage_child',
    successorRole: a.successorRole || '宗门嫡长'
  }));

  // 3. 计算跨赛季继承资产包 (Season Heritage Package)
  const capitalBequestRate = nobleTier.seasonInheritBonus.capitalRate;
  const rawTrustMoney = clanTrust.trustFundMoney || 0;
  const carryOverTrustMoney = Math.round(rawTrustMoney * capitalBequestRate);

  const heritagePackage = {
    sourceSeasonId: seasonId,
    sourceSeasonName: currentSeason ? currentSeason.name : '汉魏风骨 · 坞壁筑基',
    targetSeasonIndex: currentSeason ? currentSeason.seasonIndex + 1 : 2,
    settlementTimestamp: Date.now(),
    nobleTier: nobleTier,
    ancestorsCount: genealogy.length,
    ancestorsSummary: ancestorsSummary,
    seasonPrestigeEarned: clanTrust.prestigeScore || 0,
    // 传世重宝永久继承
    relicsInherited: [...(clanTrust.relics || [])],
    // 宗族信托跨赛季结转金
    carryOverTrustFund: carryOverTrustMoney,
    // 传世家规祖训
    mottosCarriedForward: [...(clanTrust.mottoList || [])],
    // 下一赛季开基阶层与门第特权
    startingStatus: nobleTier.seasonInheritBonus.startingStatus,
    nextSeasonStartingPerkDesc: seasonId === 'season_2_liu_chao'
      ? (nobleTier.tier >= 3
        ? '下一赛季（南北归一·隋唐草创）起步自带关陇名门余荫与书香祖茔，直膺科第解额与开皇官爵！'
        : '下一赛季（南北归一·隋唐草创）起步获得宗亲庇护与坚实开基资产。')
      : (nobleTier.tier >= 3
        ? '下一赛季（六朝烟雨）起步自带南渡庄园与上品门阀光环，直接保送中正仕途！'
        : '下一赛季（六朝烟雨）起步获得宗亲庇护与坚实开基资产。')
  };

  return heritagePackage;
}

/**
 * 执行 200 年赛季大结卷（严格幂等，仅在玩家最终确认后执行）
 */
function settleTwoHundredYearSeason(familyProfile, seasonId = 'season_1_han_wei') {
  if (!familyProfile) throw new Error('家族档案不能为空');

  familyProfile.seasonHistory = familyProfile.seasonHistory || [];

  // 严格幂等防重：对同一赛季若已完成大结卷，直接返回已有档案，绝不多次扣费或重复入录
  const existingRecord = familyProfile.seasonHistory.find(h => h.sourceSeasonId === seasonId);
  if (existingRecord) {
    return existingRecord;
  }

  const currentSeason = getSeasonById(seasonId);
  const heritagePackage = previewSeasonSettlement(familyProfile, seasonId);

  // 4. 将赛季结算成果深固于家族历史功勋库中
  familyProfile.seasonHistory.push(heritagePackage);

  // 推进赛季索引
  familyProfile.currentSeasonId = (currentSeason && SEASONS_REGISTRY[currentSeason.seasonIndex])
    ? SEASONS_REGISTRY[currentSeason.seasonIndex].id
    : (currentSeason ? currentSeason.id : 'season_2_liu_chao');

  // 更新信托为结转后的新资金池
  familyProfile.clanTrust = familyProfile.clanTrust || {};
  familyProfile.clanTrust.trustFundMoney = heritagePackage.carryOverTrustFund;
  familyProfile.clanTrust.lastSeasonTier = heritagePackage.nobleTier.name;
  familyProfile.clanTrust.lastStartingStatus = heritagePackage.startingStatus;

  // 赛季声望重置与阶梯荣誉承继：杜绝声望永续棘轮膨胀
  // 基础底定 50 分 + 本赛季门阀品阶荣誉结转 (顶级阀阅 +80，郡望著姓 +50，郡中名士 +20)
  const prestigeCarryover = heritagePackage.nobleTier.tier >= 4 ? 80 :
    (heritagePackage.nobleTier.tier === 3 ? 50 :
    (heritagePackage.nobleTier.tier === 2 ? 20 : 0));
  familyProfile.clanTrust.prestigeScore = 50 + prestigeCarryover;

  return heritagePackage;
}

if (typeof module !== 'undefined' && module.exports) {
  module.exports = {
    CLAN_NOBLE_TIERS,
    evaluateClanNobleTier,
    previewSeasonSettlement,
    settleTwoHundredYearSeason
  };
}
